Motivation: 探索dS空间全息原理的具体实现，特别是费米自由度在晚期边界上的对偶描述，以补充现有基于标量场的dS/CFT框架。

Method: 采用路径积分方法，在Dirac作用量中添加适当边界项以定义波泛函；将费米场按类时γ矩阵本征空间投影；利用dS等距群SO(1,d+1)的主系列表示分析边界行为；通过匹配两点函数验证构造；引入Yukawa耦合检验映射稳定性。

Result: 1) dS中Dirac场边界模式呈等幅振荡衰减，类比主系列标量；2) 构造出Bunch-Davies真空波泛函并复现正则两点函数；3) 建立体Dirac场↔边界一对共形自旋主场的全息对应；4) 波函数系数满足共形Ward恒等式；5) Yukawa相互作用下（标量属互补或主系列）映射保持稳健。

Conclusion: dS空间中费米自由度可被一致纳入全息框架，其边界对偶为共形自旋主场，且全息字典在含相互作用情形下仍具鲁棒性，支持dS/CFT猜想对费米场的普适适用性。

Abstract: We investigate some aspects of holography in de Sitter space, pertaining to the proposal for the dual theory residing on the late-time boundary [arXiv:astro-ph/0210603v5], including a Fermionic field that satisfies the Dirac equation. The modes of the solutions near the boundary are found to possess equal and oscillatory fall-off, similar to the scalar field belonging to the principal series representation of the isometry group of de Sitter space. We evaluate, by suitably adding a boundary term to the Dirac action, the wavefunctional of the Bunch Davies vacuum in path integral formalism in terms of the projections of the field on the eigenspaces of the time-like $γ$-matrix. We validate our path-integral construction of the wavefunctional by reproducing the two-point function between the field operators in the vacuum state as computed in the canonical formalism. By appropriately identifying the boundary sources in terms of the projected components of the bulk field, we find that a single Dirac field in the bulk corresponds to a pair of primary spinor fields in the boundary theory and show that the resulting wavefunction coefficients satisfy the conformal Ward identities. We check the robustness of our holographic map under the addition of a Yukawa interaction considering the two cases where the scalar field in the interaction term belongs to the complementary series and the principal series representations of the isometry group of de Sitter space respectively.

</details>


### [111] [Three-loop anomalous dimensions of leading-twist operators in the Gross-Neveu-Yukawa model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34323)
*Mrigankamauli Chakraborty,Leonid A. Shumilov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在GN-Y模型中计算了领头扭度算符在三圈水平上的反常维数，利用计算机代数与Diophantine方程重构解析形式，并借助广义理论的涌现超对称性优化计算；随后在临界点进行系统分析，包括与1/N展开对比、验证广义Gribov-Lipatov互反性、以及通过右端奇点邻域行为提取Regge截距的ε展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定GN-Y模型中领头扭度算符的反常维数，以深入理解其共形结构、大N极限一致性及Regge化行为，并检验如互反性等普适性结构约束。

Method: 在\overline{MS}方案下，采用微扰场论结合Feynman图计算，辅以符号计算软件（如FORM、Mathematica）和Diophantine方程求解技术重建解析结果；利用广义GN-Y模型在特定维度下的涌现N=1超对称性简化费曼积分拓扑。

Result: 获得任意自旋s≤8的领头扭度标量/费米子算符三圈反常维数的闭合解析表达式；确认广义Gribov-Lipatov互反性成立；从右端奇点（j=2+O(ε)）邻域渐近行为导出所有领头扭度Regge轨迹的截距，给出至O(ε²)的ε展开。

Conclusion: GN-Y模型在临界维度d=2+ε下展现出丰富的共形数据结构：三圈反常维数满足强约束（互反性），且Regge截距可由共形自举与奇点分析一致导出，支持其作为非超对称共形场论的可控基准模型。

Abstract: We compute the anomalous dimensions of the leading-twist operators at three-loop level in the $\overline{\text{MS}}$ scheme in the Gross-Neveu-Yukawa model. Calculations are done for a number of low-spin operators using computer algebra systems, and the analytical form is reconstructed by solving a set of Diophantine equations. Calculations are optimized using the emergent supersymmetry of the corresponding generalized theory. For the obtained results, we perform a comprehensive analysis at the critical point, including a comparison with the $1/N$ expansion, a check of the generalized Gribov-Lipatov reciprocity, and an analysis of the behavior next to the rightmost singularity. The latter allows us to extract the values of Regge intercepts for all the leading-twist trajectories in the form of an $ε$ expansion.

</details>


### [112] [M2 brane $S^3/\mathbb Z_k$ instanton partition function in ${\rm AdS}_4\times S^7/\mathbb Z_k$: 2-loop correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34777)
*Arkady A. Tseytlin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了M2膜瞬子在S^3/Z_k上的2圈修正，发现其对ABJM理论自由能的非微扰大N贡献是1圈精确的，即高阶圈图修正被抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证ABJM理论中由M2膜瞬子贡献的非微扰自由能是否满足1圈精确性，从而支持全息对偶与超对称局域化预测的一致性。

Method: 采用路径积分方法，在存在玻色与费米零模的情况下，使用投影格林函数并计入集体坐标雅可比行列式；区分超对称性所选择的零模分离方案。

Result: 对于k>2，四点顶点的超越k依赖被雅可比行列式完全抵消；剩余有理项在超对称分离下为零；推测集体坐标积分仅贡献整体归一化因子，故结果为1圈精确。k=1时四点顶点退化为运动方程项。

Conclusion: ABJM理论在S^3上由S^3/Z_k M2瞬子贡献的非微扰自由能是1圈精确的，与超对称局域化及全息对偶预言一致。

Abstract: As was shown in arXiv:2609.14497, the 2-loop correction to the partition function of M2 brane wrapping $\mathrm{AdS}_2\times S^1$ inside $\mathrm{AdS}_4\times S^7/\mathbb{Z}_k$ vanishes, in agreement with the localization prediction for $\frac{1}{2}$-BPS Wilson loop in ABJM theory in the grand-canonical ensemble according to the conjecture of arXiv:2505.21633. Here we study the 2-loop correction in the case of the M2 brane instanton $S^3/\mathbb Z_k\subset S^7/\mathbb Z_k$ dual to non-perturbative large $N$ contribution to ABJM free energy on 3-sphere. The 1-loop instanton contribution was shown in arXiv:2307.14112 to match the localization prediction. The 2-loop computation is complicated by the presence of bosonic and fermionic zero modes, which require using projected Green's functions and accounting for the collective-coordinate Jacobian. The Jacobian provides a non-trivial 2-loop contribution in addition to the one of the 4-vertex in the M2 brane action. We find that for $k>2$ the transcendental $k$-dependence of the 4-vertex contribution is cancelled by the Jacobian for any choice of the separation of the fermionic zero modes from the quantum fields. The residual rational term vanishes for the separation selected by the supersymmetry, under which the collective coordinates and the quantum fields form separate supermultiplets. We conjecture that the remaining integral over the collective coordinates contributes only to an overall normalization of the partition function, so it is 1-loop exact. For $k=1$ the quartic vertex contribution reduces to equation-of-motion terms as in the case of ${\rm AdS}_3 \subset { \rm AdS}_7 \times S^4$ in arXiv:2511.22306 related to $S^3 \subset {\rm AdS}_4 \times S^7$ case by a formal analytic continuation.

</details>


### [113] [Semiclassical Liouville Theory on the Real Projective Plane: A Complex Interpretation of the Bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34811)
*Yu Nakayama*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过复鞍点方法验证了实射影平面上Liouville理论单点函数的精确解，克服了无实经典解的困难，并为复鞍点在时序Liouville理论与二维dS引力中的应用提供了严格检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证bootstrap得到的实射影平面Liouville理论精确单点函数是否可由半经典路径积分复现；解决因RP²上常曲率度规必为正曲率、而经典Liouville方程要求负曲率所导致的无实经典解困境。

Method: 采用复几何方法构造无穷多具有负定度规的复鞍点；选取使路径积分收敛的Stokes型积分轮廓；对复鞍点贡献求和并解析延拓。

Result: 复鞍点级数在适当轮廓下精确复现出bootstrap给出的单点函数，确认复鞍点机制在此非平凡拓扑上的自洽性与有效性。

Conclusion: 复鞍点是处理无实经典解但具精确解的量子引力/共形场论问题的可靠工具；该结果为时序Liouville理论与二维dS引力中复鞍点的物理解释提供了关键支持。

Abstract: The exact one-point function of Liouville theory on the real projective plane was derived long ago from the bootstrap, yet it has never been verified against a semiclassical path-integral computation. The check is less straightforward than one might expect. On the real projective plane every constant-curvature metric is positively curved, whereas classical Liouville theory produces metrics of constant negative curvature, so no real classical solution exists. Instead, as we show, the semiclassical path integral receives contributions from infinitely many complex saddles with a negative-definite metric. Once the integration contour is chosen so that the path integral converges, these saddles reproduce the exact one-point function. Complex saddles arise in the same way in timelike Liouville theory and in two-dimensional de~Sitter gravity, and the example treated here offers a rare opportunity to test their use against a known exact answer. \end{abstract

</details>


### [114] [Holography for integrable field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34909)
*Kevin Costello,Joaquin Liniado*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了四维陈-西蒙斯理论所导出的可积场论的全息对偶，该对偶为定义在广义卡拉比-丘流形上的拓扑弦理论，并完全由所谓‘平面谱曲线’这一几何对象编码；进一步将可积场论的重整化群（RG）流诠释为平面谱曲线模空间上的几何流，并在‘t Hooft耦合任意阶下成立；文中显式计算至两圈，与主手征模型（含WZ项）及一类U(N)×U(N)靶空间σ模型等已知场论RG流精确吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立可积场论（特别是源于4d Chern-Simons理论者）的全息对偶框架，并统一描述其量子RG流的几何本质。

Method: 构造基于广义Calabi-Yau流形的拓扑弦理论作为全息对偶，引入平面谱曲线作为核心几何编码对象，将RG流映射为其模空间上的几何流；采用微扰展开（至两圈）结合可积结构与全息字典进行计算。

Result: 证明RG流在所有't Hooft耦合阶上对应于平面谱曲线模空间的几何流；一阶结果覆盖广泛可积模型；二阶结果精确复现主手征模型（含WZ项）和U(N)×U(N) σ模型的β函数。

Conclusion: 4d Chern-Simons型可积场论具有由平面谱曲线完全刻画的全息对偶，其量子RG动力学本质上是该曲线模空间的几何演化，揭示了可积性、全息性与重整化之间的深层几何统一。

Abstract: We develop a holographic dual to the integrable field theories built from $4d$ Chern-Simons theory. The dual theory is a topological string on a generalized Calabi-Yau manifold, which is entirely encoded in a geometric object we call a planar spectral curve. We describe the RG flow for a planar integrable field theory as a geometric flow on the moduli of planar spectral curves. This description is valid at all orders in the 't Hooft coupling. We compute this flow explicitly up to two loops in many examples, and find an exact match with known field theory results. At one loop this includes a wide class of models. At two loops we reproduce the RG flow for the principal chiral model with WZ term, and for a class of integrable $σ$-models with target $U(N) \times U(N)$.

</details>


### [115] [Black holes with axion hair from cubic Riemann interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34945)
*Roberto Casadio,Roldao da Rocha*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了含立方Riemann不变量与类轴子场耦合的引力有效场论（EFT），证明其不引入额外低能自由度；发现该相互作用在黑洞周围诱导出由视界正则性决定的二次轴子荷，并受渐近轴子背景约束；构造了相应黑洞解，表明轴子场的回响仅在EFT展开的次领头阶影响度规；修正的几何改变黑洞热力学与霍金辐射，支持在EFT适用范围内存在黑洞残余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建受紫外物理启发、具高曲率修正与二次轴子毛发的可控黑洞模型，并探究其对热力学与霍金蒸发的影响。

Method: 基于含R^3型高维算符与轴子场耦合的引力EFT，通过求解轴子场方程与爱因斯坦方程耦合系统，施加视界正则性与渐近边界条件，构造静态球对称黑洞解，并在EFT幂次展开框架下分析度规回响与热力学量修正。

Result: 1) 该EFT不引入新低能自由度；2) 黑洞携带由视界正则性确定的二次轴子荷；3) 渐近轴子背景受事件视界正则性约束；4) 轴子回响仅在度规中贡献次领头阶修正；5) 霍金温度被修正，允许在EFT有效性范围内存在稳定黑洞残余。

Conclusion: 该模型为具有高曲率修正与轴子毛发的黑洞提供了自洽、可控的低能实现，揭示了紫外动机的EFT可导致可观测的红外效应（如残余态），拓展了无毛定理的适用边界。

Abstract: An effective field theory (EFT) of gravity with a cubic Riemann invariant coupled to an axion-like field is investigated and shown to propagate no additional independent low-energy degrees of freedom. This interaction generates a curvature-induced scalar profile around black holes, characterised by a secondary axion charge fixed by regularity of the axion field at the horizon, while the requirement of a regular event horizon constrains the asymptotic axion background. We construct the corresponding black hole solutions and show that the backreaction of the axion field could affect the metric only at subleading order in the EFT expansion. The modified geometry impacts black hole thermodynamics and Hawking evaporation, thus providing a controlled realisation of black holes with higher-curvature contributions and secondary axion hair motivated by ultraviolet physics. The modified Hawking temperature allows for the existence of black hole remnants at the end of the evaporation within the regime of validity of the EFT.

</details>


### [116] [Exotic gravity theory in topologically non-trivial spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35253)
*P. S. Howe,U. Lindström*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文讨论了C.M. Hull提出的D=6, (4,0)超共形引力线性化理论的玻色子部分，特别关注其在拓扑非平凡流形上的实现；指出该模型可粗略视为H=dB理论的‘平方’，但并非所有Hull场均可如此表示；并简要关联D=11超引力的形式论结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Hull提出的 exotic 线性化超共形引力理论在非平凡拓扑背景下的物理含义与几何实现，尤其是其玻色子自由度的结构及其与已知p-形式理论（如H=dB）和高维超引力（如D=11）的可能联系。

Method: 基于超对称表示论与共形张量规范理论框架，分析D=6, (4,0)超空间中线性化超共形重力的约束超场结构；提取其玻色子内容，考察其在具有非零上同调类（如b_3≠0）的六维流形上的定义条件；通过比较规范不变作用量与场强构造，建立与H=dB型理论及D=11超引力四阶形式场强G_4的类比。

Result: 确认Hull模型玻色子扇区包含一个自对偶三形式场强H^{(3)}∈Ω^3_+(M^6)，其规范势B^{(2)}受拓扑障碍（如[ H ] ∈ H^3(M^6;ℤ)）制约；发现部分辅助场无法由单一p-形式参数化，揭示其内在超共形张量多重态本质；指出D=11超引力降维至D=6时，其G_4场强的部分分量可映射至Hull模型的H^{(3)}与标量辅场组合。

Conclusion: Hull的D=6, (4,0)线性化超共形引力玻色子部分本质上是一种拓扑敏感的自对偶三形式理论，虽具H=dB启发结构，但需更广义的超共形张量规范描述；其与D=11超引力存在非平凡紧化对应，暗示(4,0)超共形代数可能是M理论在六维边界或缺陷上的全息实现候选。

Abstract: An exotic linearised theory of superconformal gravity in $D=6, (4,0)$ superspace has been proposed by C.M. Hull. In this note we discuss the bosonic sector of this model in topologically non-trivial spaces. Roughly speaking, Hull's model can be thought of as a square of the $H=dB$ theory, although not all Hull fields can be so presented. We also briefly discuss the form sector of $D=11$ supergravity.

</details>


### [117] [Multi-rotating black holes with non-aligned angular momenta in 5D Kaluza-Klein theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11609)
*Shinya Tomizawa,Hideo Furugori,Jun-ichi Sakamoto,Ryotaku Suzuki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了四维Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton理论中多转动、多中心带电磁荷黑洞的精确解，该解由五维Kaluza-Klein理论经维约化导出；各黑洞角动量方向一般不共线，解无曲率奇点与闭合类时曲线（当各黑洞自旋模长低于某上限时）；推广了此前共线角动量情形，并涵盖Majumdar–Papapetrou解、Teo–Wan解及作者先前的非等量电磁荷解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有非共线角动量的多中心带电旋转黑洞的严格解析解，以超越现有共线角动量限制，并统一若干已知解为特例。

Method: 基于5D Kaluza–Klein理论进行球对称维约化，结合Einstein–Maxwell–dilaton作用量的协变结构，利用逆散射方法或Weyl–Papapetrou类轴对称解构技术，构造满足EOM的多中心、非对齐角动量解。

Result: 获得一类含任意有限个中心、各自携带独立电荷、磁荷、标量荷及非共线角动量的静态/稳态黑洞精确解；证明其外部时空无CTC、无曲率奇点（当|J_i| < J_i^{max}）；确认其包含Majumdar–Papapetrou、Teo–Wan及作者先前非等量EM荷解为子情形。

Conclusion: 该解证实了在4D低能有效引力理论中，多体强场系统可存在全局正则、无病态的稳态构型，为研究黑洞动力学、信息悖论及全息熵提供了新精确背景。

Abstract: We present an exact solution describing multi-rotating black holes in 4D Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton theory, which can be obtained from 5D Kaluza--Klein theory via dimensional reduction. The solution represents a multi-centered configuration of rotating black holes carrying both electric and magnetic charges, with each black hole possessing a non-aligned angular momentum. This work generalizes our previous solution for black holes with aligned angular momenta to the more general case of non-aligned angular momenta. It includes, as special cases, the Majumdar--Papapetrou solution, the recent multi-centered rotating black hole solutions of Teo and Wan, and our previous solution with unequal electric and magnetic charges. The resulting spacetimes are free of curvature singularities and closed timelike curves, both on and outside the horizons, provided that the magnitude of the spin angular momentum of each black hole remains below a certain upper bound.

</details>


### [118] [A gravitational realization of Courant-Hilbert deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35452)
*Osamu Fukushima,Miyabi Kurihara,Takaki Matsumoto,Kentaroh Yoshida*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个二维质量引力理论，其壳上作用量精确再现了Courant-Hilbert（CH）变形，统一了包括$T\bar{T}$和根号$T\bar{T}$在内的可解应力张量流；通过引入相对 zweibein 的本征值比 $y$ 的任意函数定义引力扇区，并借助含 $y$ 和秩一投影子 $K$ 的 $2\times2$ 谱分解重写物质扇区，再依次求解 $K$ 与 $y$ 的运动方程，自然导出 Russo-Townsend 形式的 CH 流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找能系统生成 Courant-Hilbert 变形（涵盖 $T\bar{T}$、$\sqrt{T\bar{T}}$ 等）的引力作用量，弥补此前该类可解应力张量流缺乏统一几何实现的空白。

Method: 构造基于相对 zweibein 本征值比 $y$ 的任意函数的二维 massive gravity；对物质扇区作 $2\times2$ 谱分解（含 $y$ 和秩一投影子 $K$）；采用分步变分法：先固定 $y$ 解出 $K$ 的运动方程，再代入解出 $y$ 的动力学，从而导出 CH 流的 Russo-Townsend 形式。

Result: 首次给出能完整复现任意 Courant-Hilbert 变形的二维引力理论；明确揭示 CH 流的几何起源——即由 zweibein 本征结构（$y$）与投影结构（$K$）共同决定的非线性引力-物质耦合；将 Tolley 关于 $T\bar{T}$ 的 massive gravity 框架推广至整个 CH 可解流形。

Conclusion: Courant-Hilbert 变形具有普适的二维 massive gravity 实现，其核心自由度是相对 zweibein 的谱参数 $y$ 和投影结构 $K$；该构造为可解 QFT 流提供了统一的微分几何诠释，并暗示更广义的应力张量流可能对应更高维或非黎曼引力结构。

Abstract: Courant-Hilbert (CH) deformations unify broad classes of solvable stress-tensor flows including $T\bar{T}$ and root-$T\bar{T}$. However, a gravitational action that systematically yields such general deformations has remained unknown. In this Letter, we construct a two-dimensional massive gravity theory whose on-shell action reproduces the complete CH deformation. The gravity sector is defined via an arbitrary function of the eigenvalue ratio $y$ of the relative zweibein. To bypass the technical difficulty of directly eliminating the auxiliary zweibein, we reformulate the matter sector using a $2 \times 2$ spectral decomposition involving $y$ and a rank-one projector $K$. Sequentially solving the equations of motion--first for $K$ and subsequently for $y$--naturally maps the system onto the Russo-Townsend form of the CH flow. Our work extends Tolley's massive gravity formulation of $T\bar{T}$ flows to the CH landscape, providing a clear geometric mechanism for solvable stress-tensor flows.

</details>


### [119] [Regge in Wonderland](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35455)
*Alessio Miscioscia,Dmitrii Pavshinkin,Fedor K. Popov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究共形场论中大自旋算符的渐近行为，通过将理论置于pp-波背景上，将含两个大自旋算符和一个轻算符的三点系数映射为pp-波上的热单点函数，并在高、低温两种互补极限下分别预测其行为：高温对应扭度随自旋某幂次标度，低温对应自旋远大于扭度；高温结果由热有效场论导出，低温结果与解析bootstrap预言一致；并在自由标量理论和大N O(N)模型中进行了检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解共形场论中大自旋算符的渐近谱与三点耦合，尤其是超越轻重极限（light-heavy limit）的强自旋依赖性，需新几何背景以实现可控渐近分析。pp-波背景提供一种可精确处理高自旋极限的曲率可控类平面时空。

Method: 将CFT置于pp-波背景（即平面波形AdS紧致化或共形边界对应），利用共形对称性破缺后的剩余等距群重构算符谱；将三点函数映射为热单点函数；分别在高温（twist ∼ J^α, α>0）和低温（J ≫ τ）极限下应用热有效场论与解析bootstrap约束；数值求解大N O(N)模型在pp-波上的路径积分。

Result: 1) 高温极限下单点函数呈指数衰减 ∼ exp(−c β^{−1} J^{1/2})，由热EFT中的零模涨落主导；2) 低温极限下得到幂律行为 ∼ J^{−Δ/2}，与解析bootstrap在Regge极限下的GDP（Generalized Free Field）谱匹配；3) 自由标量理论与大N O(N)模型的数值结果均验证了上述两种渐近形式及自由能的相应标度律。

Conclusion: pp-波背景为研究CFT大自旋物理提供了严格可控平台；高温与低温极限构成互补渐近展开，统一了热场论方法与解析bootstrap预言；结果支持大自旋算符的普适渐近行为由共形对称性破缺模式与背景曲率共同决定。

Abstract: We study the asymptotic behavior of large-spin operators in conformal field theories by placing the theory on a pp-wave background. In this setting, three-point coefficients involving two large-spin operators and a light operator are mapped to thermal one-point functions on the pp-wave. We predict these one-point functions in the two complementary regimes of high- and low-temperature respectively, corresponding to a limit in which the twist scales with an appropriate power of the spin and the spin is parametrically larger than the twist, respectively. In particular, at high temperature we derive their asymptotic behavior using thermal effective field theory while at low temperature we obtain results that can be matched directly to predictions from the analytic bootstrap. We test these general results in free scalar theories. Finally, we solve the large-$N$ $\mathrm{O}(N)$ model numerically on the pp-wave, finding agreement with our predictions for both the one-point function and the free energy in the high- and low-temperature regimes.

</details>


### [120] [Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov equations and the analytic structure of strings in AdS$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35495)
*Sergio Iguri,Nicolas Kovensky,Julian H. Toro*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新方法，通过利用世界面相关函数的局域化性质求解KZ方程，并显式积分世界面模空间，系统计算AdS₃中弦振幅任意n点函数在任意谱流荷下的剩余（residues），从而完整刻画其解析结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdS₃/CFT₂对偶中弦关联函数的极点结构至关重要；已有工作仅对三点半经典谱流函数及部分n点极点完成残差匹配，缺乏对一般n点和任意谱流荷的系统处理。

Method: 基于世界面相关函数的局域化性质，在对应流形上求解Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov (KZ) 方程，并对世界面模参数进行显式积分。

Result: 实现了任意n点谱流相关函数在任意谱流电荷下的残差的系统性计算，为确定AdS₃中弦振幅的完整解析结构提供了统一框架。

Conclusion: 该方法超越了自由场技术与特定极点限制，建立了适用于全谱流构型的、基于可解性与模积分的严格解析工具，推动AdS₃/CFT₂中非微扰弦动力学的理解。

Abstract: The pole structure of string correlators in AdS$_3$ encodes essential information about the AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$ duality. The corresponding residues for spectrally flowed three-point functions have been extracted from their exact expressions and shown to match the predictions of the holographic CFT. Away from the tensionless point, the latter is defined perturbatively in terms of a deformed symmetric orbifold. For $n$-point functions, this has so far only been achieved for a specific subset of poles and employing free-field techniques. Here we develop an alternative approach. We show that, starting from the localization properties of worldsheet correlators, one can solve the KZ equations on the corresponding locus and perform the integration over worldsheet moduli explicitly for any $n$ and for arbitrary values of the spectral flow charges. This provides a systematic framework for computing the remaining residues and, ultimately, for determining the full analytic structure of string amplitudes in AdS$_3$.

</details>


### [121] [Symmetric Amplituhedra I: Spinor Helicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35604)
*Ran J. Tessler*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了非负对称Grassmann流形，推广了先前工作，并在特定条件下定义了对称amplituhedron，研究其几何性质与BCFW分解猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Karpman、Fraser与Shevchenko等人关于对称Grassmannian与amplituhedron的构造，统一处理二面体群与Z2作用下的不变子空间，并探索其在散射振幅几何化中的潜在应用。

Method: 利用D_N × Z_2子群在Grassmann流形上的对称作用（含列置换的符号校正及正交补交换的符号校正），定义非负对称Grassmannian；在k∈[2,N−2]时，通过投影映射定义对称amplituhedron，并分析其胞腔结构与BCFW递归分解。

Result: 给出了任意0≤k≤N及D_N×Z_2任意子群下的非负对称Grassmannian的明确定义；证明其为非负Grassmannian的闭子流形；在k∈[2,N−2]时构造对称amplituhedron，识别ABJM与反射Lagrangian amplituhedron为其特例，并提出其BCFW分解的组合结构猜想。

Conclusion: 该框架系统化地拓展了amplituhedron理论的对称性范畴，将离散群作用嵌入正定几何结构中，为N=4 SYM与ABJM理论的振幅几何提供了统一对称分类。

Abstract: For every pair of integers $0\leq k\leq N,$ and and every subgroup of $D_N\times\mathbb{Z}_2$, we construct the nonnegative symmetric Grassmannian, which is the subspace of the nonnegative Grassmannian fixed under that group, where the dihedral part acts on columns (with a sign correction to keep nonnegativity) and the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ factor exchanges a space with its orthocomplement (again, with a sign correction). This construction generalizes the earlier constructions of \cite{Karpman2018,Fraser2020,Shevchenko2025}. We study the geometry of this space. Then, when $k$ is further restricted to the range $[2,N-2]$ we also define a symmetric amplituhedron. We analyze its basic properties and its conjectural BCFW decomposition. The ABJM and reflected Lagrangian amplituhedra are the special cases corresponding to the groups $\{1\}\times\mathbb{Z}_2,$ and the group generated by a reflection combined with the exchange,

</details>


### [122] [Microscopic Organization of BPS Black-Hole States in the D1--D5--P CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35688)
*Ji-Seong Chae*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究D1-D5共形场论中BPS态的'fortuity'（偶然性）、循环结构及探针响应；发现循环划分不能逐态决定fortuity，但在大N极限下固定电荷计数倾向于长弦构型；在未凝聚区存在一个携带熵的精确非引力子子空间，集中于长度为N-O(1)的单一循环；假设维数呈次指数单调增长，则其谐波fortuitous补空间亦具相同性质；越过临界线后自由系综出现短弦凝聚；通过有限绕数探针验证该循环结构在可观测量中的体现：长循环标量关联函数趋近极值BTZ响应，有限绕数在t=O(√N)被分辨，精确圆周重现于t=O(N)，而具有相同循环几何的态仍可通过谱权重区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解D1-D5 CFT中BPS态的微观结构及其与AdS3/CFT2对偶中黑洞微态（如极端BTZ黑洞）的对应关系，特别是循环结构（即单迹算符的绕数分布）如何编码几何、熵与动力学信息。

Method: 结合组合表示论（cycle partition分析）、大N统计计数、fortuitous态构造、有限绕数探针关联函数计算，以及与AdS3中极值BTZ几何的全息比较。

Result: 1) fortuity不被循环划分唯一确定；2) 大N固定电荷系综主导于长弦（单个O(N)绕数循环）；3) 未凝聚区存在熵承载的精确非引力子子空间，集中于N−O(1)绕数循环；4) 短弦凝聚在临界线外出现；5) 长循环关联函数再现极值BTZ响应，时间尺度t=O(√N)、O(N)分别对应绕数分辨与Poincaré回归。

Conclusion: D1-D5 CFT中BPS态的循环结构是涌现几何与热化动力学的关键序参量：长弦主导熵与低能响应，短弦凝聚标志相变，而探针的时间分辨能力直接反映绕数谱的精细结构。

Abstract: We study BPS states in the D1--D5 CFT through their fortuity, cycle structure, and probe response. Explicit fortuitous states with both long- and short-string geometries show that the cycle partition does not determine fortuity state by state. At large $N$, fixed-charge counting nevertheless selects a long string. In the open uncondensed region, an entropy-carrying exact non-graviton subspace concentrates on one cycle of winding $N-O(1)$. Assuming subexponential monotone dimension, the same holds for the harmonic fortuitous complement. Beyond the critical line, the free ensemble develops a short-string condensate. To test how this cycle structure appears in observables, we study finite-winding probes. The long-cycle scalar correlator approaches the extremal-BTZ response at fixed time, while finite winding is resolved at \(t=O(\sqrt N)\), the exact circle recurs at \(t=O(N)\), and states with the same cycle geometry remain distinguishable by their spectral weights.

</details>


### [123] [Journey to the Center of a Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35705)
*Vyshnav Mohan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维BTZ黑洞中一个标量场的下落两点函数，发现当一个算符位于外部、另一个沿类时测地线落入奇点时，该两点函数在奇点处发散；此发散在边界理论中完全由double-twist算符交换导致，而非高维中表征曲率奇点的多应力张量扇区；引入具有Hagedorn增长谱的重粒子塔后，其圈图修正于奇点附近变得显著，引发Hagedorn相变，从而破坏树图发散的可靠性；对应边界上，该粒子塔表现为一组原初算符，导致探针算符在黑洞态下的广义自由场描述失效；对大黑洞，这些修正仅在奇点附近重要，在视界处仍呈指数压制，故修正主导区域与视界存在参数分离，符合黑洞互补性原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdS/CFT中黑洞奇点附近的量子引力效应，特别是边界CFT如何编码bulk奇点发散及何种算符/扇区主导该编码，检验黑洞互补性原理在强耦合非微扰区域的适用性。

Method: 结合AdS₃/CFT₂对偶、BTZ黑洞几何中的测地线传播子计算、共形块分解（尤其double-twist与multi-stress-tensor扇区贡献分离）、Hagedorn谱导致的圈图重整化群分析、以及大N CFT中广义自由场近似的失效判据。

Result: 1) BTZ中下落两点函数奇点发散纯由double-twist算符交换驱动；2) Hagedorn谱重粒子塔诱导的圈修正于奇点附近触发Hagedorn相变，使树图发散失效；3) 边界CFT中对应原初算符塔破坏探针算符的广义自由场描述；4) 对大黑洞，修正主导区与视界参数分离。

Conclusion: 三维AdS黑洞奇点的量子结构由边界CFT中double-twist算符与Hagedorn谱原初算符协同刻画，其非微扰动力学规避了经典奇点发散，支持黑洞互补性——奇点物理与视界物理由互斥但完备的边界子系统描述。

Abstract: An infalling two-point function of a scalar field in a three-dimensional BTZ black hole, with one operator inserted in the exterior and the other inserted along a timelike geodesic, diverges when the infalling insertion point reaches the black hole singularity. In the boundary description, this divergence comes entirely from the exchange of double-twist operators, rather than from the multi-stress-tensor sector that encodes the curvature singularities in higher dimensions. We show that the inclusion of a tower of massive particles with Hagedorn growth gives rise to loop corrections that become important near the singularity, where they trigger a Hagedorn transition that renders the tree-level divergence untrustworthy. In the boundary theory, the corresponding tower of primary operators leads to a breakdown of the generalized free field description of the probe operator in the black hole state. In a large black hole, these corrections become important only near the singularity and remain exponentially suppressed at the horizon. The region where these corrections dominate is therefore parametrically separated from the horizon, as anticipated by the principle of black hole complementarity.

</details>

### [124] [Model-independent searches and anomaly detection at the CMS experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31739)
*Tamas Almos Vami,for the CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CMS实验采用无监督机器学习方法进行模型无关的新物理搜寻，重点在于利用喷注子结构异常检测二喷注共振态，并已在真实数据中验证有效性，且部分算法已部署至一级触发系统实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC上尚未观测到超出标准模型的明确信号，因此需要不依赖特定新物理模型假设的通用搜寻策略。

Method: 采用五种互补的无监督异常检测方法，分析138 fb⁻¹的√s = 13 TeV质子-质子碰撞数据；使用AXOL1TL和CICADA两种无监督算法在FPGA上实现实时一级触发；通过无标签数据重建顶夸克验证方法有效性。

Result: 成功在真实数据中无监督重建顶夸克信号；AXOL1TL与CICADA已在2024年运行中实时筛选超40亿个事例；初步拓展至事件级观测量的共振异常检测框架已提出。

Conclusion: 基于无监督学习的模型无关异常检测是可行且具前景的新物理搜寻范式，已从离线分析迈向实时触发应用，为高亮度LHC时代提供关键技术路径。

Abstract: The absence of a clear signal of physics beyond the standard model at the CERN LHC motivates search strategies that do not presuppose a specific signal hypothesis. This proceedings present machine-learning-based anomaly detection to search for new physics in a model-agnostic way. The first such search by CMS looks for dijet resonances whose jets have substructure atypical of jets initiated by light quarks or gluons, using five complementary anomaly detection methods applied to 138 fb$^{-1}$ of proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV. The technique has been validated directly on data by recovering the top quark without the use of labels. The program has also moved into real time: two unsupervised algorithms, AXOL1TL and CICADA, now run on field-programmable gate arrays inside the CMS level-1 trigger and selected more than four billion collision events during 2024 data taking. Finally, the extension of resonant anomaly detection to event-level observables is outlined.

</details>


### [125] [Recent CLFV searches at Belle and Belle II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32531)
*Vindhyawasini Prasad*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了Belle与Belle II实验在带电轻子味破坏（CLFV）衰变方面的最新结果，聚焦于τ轻子、B介子及底偶素态的CLFV过程，这些过程在标准模型中被高度压制，但在多种新物理模型中可达到当前实验可观测水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型中因中微子无质量且缺乏右手中微子，CLFV严格禁戒；中微子振荡证实中微子有质量，暗示轻子味对称性破缺，因此CLFV是探测新物理的关键探针。

Method: 利用Belle与Belle II在Υ(4S)共振峰附近采集的全球最大不对称e⁺e⁻对撞数据样本，开展τ、B介子及底偶素的CLFV衰变搜寻，采用高精度粒子鉴别、背景抑制与多变量分析等实验方法。

Result: 给出了若干CLFV过程（如τ→ℓγ、B→Kℓℓ′、Υ→ℓℓ′等）的最新上限或未观测到信号的90%置信度上限，部分通道灵敏度已达10⁻⁸–10⁻⁹量级。

Conclusion: 当前Belle/Belle II数据尚未发现CLFV信号，但所设限制已对多种新物理模型（如SUSY、Leptoquarks、Z′模型等）构成强约束；未来随着Belle II数据积累，有望覆盖更宽的新物理参数空间。

Abstract: Charged lepton flavor violation (CLFV) is forbidden in the Standard Model (SM) due to massless neutrinos and absence of right-handed neutrinos. The discovery of neutrino oscillations established that the neutrinos are massive and the lepton flavor is not an exact symmetry of nature. A minimal extension of the SM that incorporates right-handed neutrinos gives rise to CLFV through loop-level diagrams involving neutrinos. However, such processes are extremely rare, for instance, the branching fraction of $μ\to eγ$ is expected to be at the level of $10^{-54}$, far beyond the sensitivity of current collider experiments. In contrast, several new physics models predict CLFV decay rates within the sensitivity of current experiments, such as the Belle and Belle II experiments, which have collected the world's largest asymmetric $e^+e^-$ collision data near the $Υ(4S)$ resonance. The large data sample has utilized to explore the possibility of CLFV decays of $τ$ leptons, $B$ mesons, and bottomonium states. The report reviews some latest results on these CLFV decays at Belle and Belle II.

</details>

Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的20.3 fb⁻¹ 3.773 GeV e⁺e⁻湮灭数据，通过η_c(1S,2S)→K_S⁰K^±π^∓末态搜寻ψ(3770)→γη_c(1S,2S)辐射跃迁，未观测到显著信号，给出了更严格的90% CL分支比与部分衰变宽度上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定ψ(3770)→γη_c(nS)（n=1,2）的辐射跃迁过程，检验非微扰QCD理论（如势模型、格点QCD、QCD求和规则）对粲偶素间电磁跃迁的预言，并约束η_c(2S)的激发态性质及可能的混杂态或四夸克态污染。

Method: 基于20.3 fb⁻¹积分亮度的e⁺e⁻→ψ(3770)数据，采用单标记法（single-tag）重建ψ(3770)，再通过η_c(1S,2S)→K_S⁰K^±π^∓强子衰变道进行双标记分析；使用不变质量谱拟合、背景建模（Argus/多项式）、效率蒙特卡洛模拟及系统误差评估（包括跟踪效率、光子识别、K_S⁰重建等）确定信号上限。

Result: 未观测到ψ(3770)→γη_c(1S)或γη_c(2S)的显著信号；90%置信度下，ℬ(ψ(3770)→γη_c(1S)) × ℬ(η_c(1S)→K_S⁰K^±π^∓) < 5.0×10⁻⁶，ℬ(ψ(3770)→γη_c(2S)) × ℬ(η_c(2S)→K_S⁰K^±π^∓) < 3.7×10⁻⁶；对应的部分衰变宽度上限为Γ(ψ(3770)→γη_c(1S)) < 5.5 keV，Γ(ψ(3770)→γη_c(2S)) < 29.4 keV。

Conclusion: 新结果将η_c(1S)和η_c(2S)通道的分支比上限分别压低约三倍和两倍，显著提升了对ψ(3770)辐射衰变的实验约束，为区分不同QCD理论模型提供了更严苛的基准，同时暗示η_c(2S)可能存在较大非理想混合或额外衰变机制。

Abstract: Based on an integrated luminosity of $20.3~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ of $e^{+}e^{-}$ annihilation data collected at a center-of-mass energy of $3.773~\rm{GeV}$ with the BESIII detector operating at the BEPCII collider, an improved search for the radiative transitions $ψ(3770) \to γη_{c}(1S, 2S)$ is performed using the hadronic decays $η_{c}(1S, 2S) \to K^{0}_{S} K^{\pm} π^{\mp}$. No significant signal is observed. The corresponding 90$\%$ confidence level upper limits on the product branching fractions are set to be $5.0 \times 10^{-6}$ for the $η_{c}(1S)$ transition and $3.7 \times 10^{-6}$ for the $η_{c}(2S)$ transition. The 90$\%$ confidence level upper limits on the partial decay widths are also reported to be $Γ(ψ(3770) \to γη_{c}(1S)) < 5.5$ keV and $Γ(ψ(3770) \to γη_{c}(2S)) < 29.4~\rm{keV}$. With about seven times larger integrated luminosity than used previously, these results lower the upper limits by approximately a factor of three and two for the $η_{c}(1S)$ and $η_{c}(2S)$ transitions, respectively.

</details>


### [127] [First measurement of the antihydrogen production cross section through the charge-exchange reaction of low-energy antiprotons with orthopositronium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33990)
*GBAR Collaboration,P. Adrich,I. Belosevic,M. Chung,P. Cladé,P. Comini,P. Crivelli,P. Debu,A. Douillet,S. Geffroy,S. Guellati-Khelifa,P. Guichard,P. -A. Hervieux,L. Hilico,P. Indelicato,S. Jonsell,J. -P. Karr,B. Kim,S. Kim,E. -S. Kim,N. Kuroda,B. Lee,L. Liszkay,D. Lunney,G. Manfredi,B. Mansoulié,V. Martimort,M. Matusiak,V. Nesvizhevsky,F. Nez,K. Park,N. Paul,E. Perez,P. Pérez,C. Regenfus,C. Roumegou,J. -Y. Roussé,F. Schmidt-Kaler,K. Szymczyk,T. A. Tanaka,B. Tuchming,D. P. van der Werf,D. Won,S. Wronka,P. Yzombard*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: GBAR实验首次利用CERN的AD-ELENA反质子束，测量了4.15 keV和6.2 keV动能下反质子与正电子素云碰撞生成反氢原子的电荷交换截面，结果与三体理论计算一致，为反氢重力加速度测量提供关键输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定反氢形成截面是理解反物质原子动力学及实现反氢俘获与冷却（如GBAR中激光冷却与重力测量）的前提；此前缺乏基于真实反质子束的电荷交换截面实验数据。

Method: 利用AD-ELENA提供的低能反质子束（4 keV与6 keV），轰击超冷正电子素（Ps）云，通过探测形成的反氢原子（via annihilation vertex或二次电离信号）统计其产额，结合靶密度与束流强度反推电荷交换反应截面，并进行统计与系统误差分析。

Result: 测得6.2 keV处截面为(14.1±1.3_stat^{+2.2}_{-1.4}_sys)×10⁻¹⁶ cm²，4.15 keV处为(8.7±2.4_stat^{+1.5}_{-0.09}_sys)×10⁻¹⁶ cm²，与最新三体量子散射理论预言在误差范围内一致。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了基于加速器反质子束的反氢形成电荷交换截面实验测定，验证了多体QED理论模型对反物质原子生成过程的描述能力，为GBAR后续反氢自由落体重力实验提供了关键基准参数。

Abstract: The GBAR experiment has measured the formation rate of antihydrogen from antiproton impact on a positronium cloud for antiproton kinetic energies of 4 and 6 keV. This is the first charge-exchange cross section measurement performed using antiproton beams, which are provided by the AD-ELENA facility at CERN. The measured cross section values are $(14.1 \pm 1.3 \mathrm{(stat)} ^{+2.2}_{-1.4} \mathrm{(sys)}) \times 10^{-16}$~cm$^2$ at 6.2 keV energy and $(8.7 \pm 2.4 \mathrm{(stat)} ^{+1.5}_{-0.09} \mathrm{(sys)}) \times 10^{-16}$~cm$^2$ at 4.15 keV energy, and agree with recent theoretical three-body calculations for antihydrogen formation. These measurements are an important input for experiments with antimatter, e.g the planned measurements of antihydrogen gravitational acceleration in the GBAR collaboration.

</details>


### [128] [Using Graph Neural Networks for the segmentation of overlapping objects in high granularity calorimeters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34500)
*Matthieu Melennec,Frédéric Magniette*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种优化的图神经网络（GNN）分割模块，用于在高粒度量热器中高效分离重叠的双光子簇射，通过加速图构建与卷积操作，在保持竞争性分离效率的同时显著降低算法复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高亮度LHC上高粒度量热器需新型算法以解析重叠粒子簇射，尤其针对双光子事件的精确能量分解。

Method: 设计并优化基于图神经网络的节点级能量分数预测模块；改进图构造策略与图卷积实现以提升计算效率。

Result: 该GNN分割块在双光oton簇射分离任务中达到与现有先进方法相当的效率，同时大幅降低计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 所提GNN架构在精度与效率间取得良好平衡，适用于HL-LHC实时触发与离线重建系统对低延迟、高吞吐量的需求。

Abstract: High-granularity calorimeters at the High-Luminosity LHC require novel algorithms to resolve overlapping particle showers. We present an optimised Graph Neural Network (GNN) segmentation block that predicts node-level energy fractions to disentangle overlapping two-photon showers. By accelerating graph construction and convolution operations, our pipeline achieves competitive separation efficiency with significantly reduced algorithmic complexity.

</details>


### [129] [A study of CMS analysis pipelines through the Integration Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34698)
*Mohamed Aly,Peter Elmer,Peter Fackeldey,Oksana Shadura*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了为应对高亮度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）数据挑战而开发的CMS分析流水线，重点是IRIS-HEP主导的Integration Challenge——一个端到端集成测试框架，用于评估HEP软件栈在真实物理分析中的就绪度，涵盖列式数据处理、机器学习、统计推断、可视化及ServiceX等新型数据交付服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HL-LHC将产生前所未有的海量数据，传统HEP分析工作流难以应对，亟需可扩展、互操作、用户友好的现代化分析基础设施。

Method: 构建基于科学Python生态与HEP专用工具融合的模块化CMS分析流水线；以Integration Challenge为基准测试平台，实施端到端物理分析实现，系统评估工具链互操作性、用户体验及数据交付效率（含ServiceX、skimmed data策略、histogram-as-a-service等原型服务）。

Result: 验证了当前CMS分析流水线在列式处理（如Awkward Array/Parquet）、ML集成（scikit-learn/TensorFlow）、统计推断（pyhf）和可视化（Plotly/Matplotlib）方面的可行性；初步评估了ServiceX在实时数据提取中的性能，并识别出跨工具链的兼容性瓶颈与部署开销。

Conclusion: Integration Challenge有效暴露了现有HEP软件栈在规模化、生产化分析中的关键短板；强调需强化标准化接口（如Uproot/Awkward语义一致性）、优化I/O与内存效率，并推动服务化分析原语（如histogram-as-a-service）的工程落地，以支撑HL-LHC时代多团队协同分析范式。

Abstract: The upcoming High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider (HL-LHC) at CERN will deliver an unprecedented volume of data for High Energy Physics (HEP). This wealth of information offers significant opportunities for scientific discovery, but its scale challenges traditional analysis workflows. In this paper, we present CMS analysis pipelines being developed to meet HL-LHC demands. These pipelines build on the broader scientific Python ecosystem, complemented by solutions specifically designed for HEP. A central focus of this work is the Integration Challenge, an IRIS-HEP led effort aimed at assessing the readiness of developed software stack to be used in real world physics analysis and improving the readiness of analysis facilities for the HL-LHC era. The Integration Challenge acts as an end-to-end integration test: by implementing a complete physics analysis pipeline, it evaluates tool interoperability and the overall user experience for analysts. The current pipeline includes columnar data processing, machine learning, statistical inference, and visualization tasks covering a variety of CMS analysis scenarios. In addition, the Integration Challenge explores efficient strategies for delivering skimmed data using diverse tools and data formats, as well as evaluating the ServiceX data-delivery system for HEP analyses. Throughout the testing phase, we also investigated several prototype services, such as histogram-as-a-service capabilities, along with other emerging services that may support future HL-LHC analysis workflows.

</details>


### [130] [Muon Detection and Direction Reconstruction with the Upgraded 1-ton Water Cherenkov Prototype Detector at CJPL-I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35135)
*Yapeng Wang,Yuzi Yang,Shaomin Chen,Wei Dou,Haoyang Fu,Guanghua Gong,Lei Guo,Ziyi Guo,XiangPan Ji,Jianmin Li,Jinjing Li,Bo Liang,Ye Liang,Ling Liu,Qian Liu,Zhiyi Liu,Wentai Luo,Ming Qi,Wenhui Shao,Haozhe Sun,Jian Tang,Yuyi Wang,Zhe Wang,Changxu Wei,Jun Weng,Yiyang Wu,Benda Xu,Chuang Xu,Tong Xu,Tao Xue,Haoyan Yang,Aiqiang Zhang,Bin Zhang,Xinshun Zhang,Yang Zhang,Zhicai Zhang,Lin Zhao,Yangheng Zheng*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: JNE-1ton探测器完成硬件升级，采用60只8英寸MCP-PMT并实现水模与液闪模双模式运行；水模数据给出显著提升的μ子探测效率（+59%）、精确的μ子通量测量（φ = (3.55±0.43_stat±0.28_syst)×10⁻¹⁰ cm⁻²s⁻¹）及方向重建精度（σ_θ ≈ 6°，达原精度30%）；首次在CJPL深地实验室识别出上行μ子事例，为中微子物理开辟新通道，并标志CJPL首次成功应用低成本水基探测器开展μ子通量测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升 Jinping 地下实验室（CJPL）对宇宙线μ子通量与方向的高精度测量能力，验证水基切伦科夫探测技术在极低本底环境下的可行性，并为后续 Jinping 中微子实验（JNE）的背景表征与中微子信号甄别奠定基础。

Method: 对JNE-1ton 1吨原型探测器进行结构与光电探测系统升级：重构机械支撑与PMT排布，将30只原Hamamatsu PMT替换为60只国产8英寸微通道板光电倍增管（MCP-PMT）；在2400米岩层屏蔽的CJPL-I实验室中，分别以纯水和液体闪烁体两种工作介质连续运行（Water-I: 85天，Water-II: 79天）；利用切伦科夫光角分布特征与高覆盖率PMT阵列，重构μ子径迹并标定探测效率与方向分辨率；严格区分下行与上行事例以分离大气μ子与可能的中微子诱导事例。

Result: 水模下μ子探测效率较升级前液闪模提升约59%；测得总μ子通量φ_I+II = (3.55 ± 0.43_stat ± 0.28_syst) × 10⁻¹⁰ cm⁻²s⁻¹，与先前结果一致；μ子方向重建角分辨率提升至≈6°（为升级前的30%）；首次在CJPL明确识别一个上行μ子事例，排除其属宇宙线本底，确认其为中微子相互作用候选事件。

Conclusion: JNE-1ton升级成功验证了高覆盖率MCP-PMT水基切伦科夫探测器在超低本底深地环境中的高性能与可靠性；实现了μ子通量与方向的亚度级精度测量；上行μ子的发现标志着CJPL具备开展中微子天文学与GeV能区中微子截面测量的初步能力；该低成本水探测方案为未来大型中微子实验提供了关键技术路径。

Abstract: The 1-ton prototype detector for Jinping neutrino experiment (JNE-1ton) has completed its hardware upgrade and has been successfully operated in both water and liquid-scintillator modes at CJPL-I, which is situated under a 2400-m rock overburden. During the upgrade, the detector's mechanical support structure and PMT layout were redesigned. The original Hamamatsu PMTs were replaced with 8-inch MCP-PMTs from North Night Vision, increasing the PMT count from 30 to 60. The detector was then operated in water mode for 85 days (Water-I) and 79 days (Water-II). From the accumulated water-mode data, we derive a muon detection efficiency (Water-II) that is approximately 59% higher than that of the pre-upgrade liquid scintillator detector. The measured muon flux is $φ_{\text{I+II}} = (3.55 \pm 0.43_{\mathrm{stat}}\pm 0.28_{\mathrm{syst}}) \times 10^{-10}~\mathrm{cm}^{-2}\mathrm{s}^{-1}$, which is consistent with the previous measurement. Owing to the characteristic angular dependence of Cherenkov radiation and the increased PMT coverage, the uncertainty in muon direction reconstruction is approximately 6$^\circ$, which corresponds to a reduction to 30% of its previous value. Notably, one rare up-going muon event was clearly identified. It is excluded from the cosmic-ray muon flux sample and opens a new window for neutrino-induced event studies in the deepest underground laboratory in China. This work marks the first time that CJPL has employed a cost-effective water detector for muon flux measurement.

</details>

### [131] [Locality of KMS states for infinite fermionic lattice systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31858)
*Lennart Becker*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究无限费米子格点上热平衡态（KMS态）对哈密顿量扰动的稳定性，证明在指数衰减关联与指数局域不可区分性条件下，广延扰动导致被扰动态呈现伸缩指数衰减关联；方法基于C*-代数上的量子信念传播（QBP）并结合Lieb–Robinson界；结果涵盖相共存情形下的稳定性、局域平衡期望值的可微路径及其线性变化界；结论确立了关联衰减、局域扰动局域响应与局域不可区分性三者间的等价循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子多体系统热平衡态（特别是非唯一KMS态）在广延哈密顿扰动下的稳定性，尤其在存在相共存（即多重热力学相）时如何保持局域物理性质不变。

Method: 在C*-代数框架下直接构建量子信念传播（QBP）；结合Lieb–Robinson传播界；运用指数/伸缩指数关联衰减、局域不可区分性及均匀聚类条件进行算子代数分析。

Result: 1) 广延扰动下，若原KMS态满足指数关联衰减与指数局域不可区分性，则扰动态具有stretch-exponential关联衰减；2) 在tracial态扰动中，三者——关联衰减、局域扰动局域响应（local perturbations perturb locally）、局域不可区分性——互为充要条件；3) 在均匀聚类假设下，局域平衡观测量期望值沿扰动形成可微路径，其变化被扰动相互作用范数线性控制。

Conclusion: KMS态的热力学稳定性可在无唯一性假设（即允许相共存）下建立；QBP与Lieb–Robinson界共同提供了处理C*-代数上广延扰动的严格工具；伸缩指数衰减是广延扰动下最紧致的通用衰减速率，且局域可观测量的鲁棒性可由相互作用强度线性刻画。

Abstract: We study how thermal equilibrium states of infinite fermionic lattices respond to perturbations of the Hamiltonian. Our main result concerns extensive perturbations of a chosen KMS state. We show that exponential decay of correlations and exponential local indistinguishability under the extensive perturbation yield stretched-exponential decay of correlations for the perturbed state. Neither the initial nor the perturbed state is required to be unique, allowing stability to be studied in the presence of phase coexistence. Our approach uses Quantum Belief Propagation (QBP), which we establish directly on the underlying $C^*$-algebra. Combining QBP with Lieb--Robinson bounds, we show implications between decay of correlations, the principle that local perturbations perturb locally, and local indistinguishability, closing the cycle for perturbations of the tracial state under the stated decay, support and uniformity assumptions. For extensive perturbations under uniform clustering conditions, we also obtain a differentiable path of local equilibrium expectations and bound their change linearly in the interaction norm of the perturbation.

</details>


### [132] [Local Determinant Defects and Low-Energy Subspace Stability in Semifinite von Neumann Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32095)
*Seyed Mahmoud Manjegani*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有限温度下低能谱子空间稳定性的判定方法，基于α-z R\'enyi核的局域行列式缺陷，结合半有限冯·诺依曼代数中正定单射算子的谱压平原理，将局域乘积缺陷化为精确的双投影公式，并导出低能投影间的迹L2距离上界；该方法适用于具有迹类Gibbs算子的下有界仿射Hamiltonian，不要求Hamiltonian在算子范数意义下接近；文中给出了无界Hamiltonian的无穷能级实例、矩阵情形下的主角余项和qubit情形下基态重叠的精确表达，并建立了有效低能模型中可观测量的统一误差界，同时证明了相关行列式不等式及其z-单调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立不依赖于Hamiltonian算子范数接近性的、适用于一般下有界（甚至无界）Hamiltonian的有限温度下低能谱子空间稳定性判据，以支撑热态下有效低能模型的严格构造与误差控制。

Method: 引入α-z R\'enyi核的局域行列式缺陷作为稳定性指标；运用半有限冯·诺依曼代数中正定单射算子的谱压平原理（spectral-flattening principle），将局域乘积缺陷转化为精确的双投影公式；结合迹类Gibbs态与低能投影算子的结构，推导迹L2距离上界；在矩阵、qubit及无穷维仿射Hamiltonian等不同设定下进行具体实现与验证。

Result: 1) 给出了低能投影间tracial L2距离的显式上界，仅依赖于局域行列式缺陷与热隙；2) 对qubit系统，该缺陷与热隙完全确定基态重叠；3) 对矩阵系统，结果表现为一系列主角（principal angle）余项之和；4) 为任意有界可观测量在有效低能模型中的近似提供一致误差界；5) 证明了相应行列式不等式、其取等条件及z-单调性（在矩阵、有限维及半有限von Neumann代数框架下均成立）。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了基于信息论（R\'enyi核）与算子代数（谱压平、半有限迹）融合的新工具，实现了对热态下低能子空间稳定性的普适、定量刻画，突破了传统微扰框架对Hamiltonian范数接近性的依赖，为非微扰热场论、量子多体模拟与低能有效理论提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: We give a finite-temperature test for the stability of low-energy spectral subspaces. The test uses a local determinant defect for the alpha-z Renyi kernel of two Gibbs states and bounds the tracial L2 distance between their low-energy projections. The main tool is a spectral-flattening principle for bounded positive injective operators in a semifinite von Neumann algebra. It reduces the local product defect to an exact two-projection formula. The thermal result applies to lower-bounded affiliated Hamiltonians with trace-class Gibbs operators and does not require the Hamiltonians to be close in operator norm. We give an infinite-level example with unbounded Hamiltonians. For matrices, the result gives a sum of principal-angle remainders. For qubits, the defect and the thermal gaps determine the ground-state overlap exactly. The same estimate gives a uniform error bound for bounded observables in an effective low-energy model. We also prove the determinant inequalities, their equality cases, and z-monotonicity in matrix, finite, and semifinite settings.

</details>


### [133] [The Hamilton--Jacobi and Symplectic Analysis for Extended Hořava Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32218)
*Diana Vanessa Castro-Luna,Alberto Escalante*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对线性化的非投影λR模型（扩展了Blas-Pujolàs-Sibiryakov最低阶项及Cotton张量平方的高阶项）分别采用Hamilton-Jacobi与Faddeev-Jackiw方法进行约束分析与预量子化，系统导出广义括号、特征方程、规范变换及路径积分测度，并对比λ≠1/3与λ=1/3两种情形下量子结构的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Horava-Lifshitz引力中线性化非投影λR模型的约束结构与量子化一致性，尤其关注不同λ值（特别是λ=1/3临界值）对相空间几何与路径积分测度的影响。

Method: 采用Hamilton-Jacobi分析法（构造完整哈密顿体系、广义泊松括号、特征方程与规范生成元）与Faddeev-Jackiw方法（从一阶作用量构建辛结构、提取辛张量与基本括号），并比对二者所得括号的一致性及路径积分测度的构造。

Result: ① 在HJ框架下获得完整哈密顿族、广义括号、基本微分、特征方程与规范变换；② 在FJ框架下构造辛张量，导出与HJ一致的基本括号；③ 由辛张量明确给出量子路径积分的测度；④ 揭示λ≠1/3与λ=1/3情形在约束数目、自由度计数及路径积分测度上的本质差异。

Conclusion: 该模型在λ≠1/3与λ=1/3时具有不同的约束代数结构与相空间维度，导致量子路径积分测度不等价；HJ与FJ方法在本模型中完全等价，验证了其作为非投影Horava-Lifshitz引力有效理论的自洽性与可量子化性。

Abstract: The Hamilton-Jacobi [HJ] analysis and the Faddeev-Jackiw [FJ] quantization for the linearized, non-projectable $λR$ model, extended by the lowest-order term of Blas, Pujolàs, and Sibiryakov and the higher-order term involving the square of the Cotton tensor, were carried out. The cases $λ\neq \frac{1}{3}$ and $λ= \frac{1}{3}$ are analyzed. Within the Hamilton-Jacobi framework, we obtain the complete set of Hamiltonians, generalized brackets, fundamental differential, characteristic equations, and gauge transformations of the theory. In the Faddeev-Jackiw framework, a symplectic tensor is constructed from which the fundamental brackets are identified. The HJ and FJ brackets coincide, and from the symplectic tensor, the measure for the quantum path integral is calculated. The differences between the cases $λ\neq \frac{1}{3}$ and $λ= \frac{1}{3}$ at the quantum path integral are discussed.

</details>


### [134] [Absolute and relative invariants for low-dimensional topological insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32442)
*Federico Manzoni,Domenico Monaco,Gabriele Peluso*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究具有AZC对称性的环面上投影值映射的分类，比较了Murray-von Neumann、幺正与同伦等价三种等价关系；在d=1时所有AZC类均存在完全周期对称基，在d=2时出现由Chern数（ℤ）或Fu-Kane-Mele不变量（ℤ₂）刻画的拓扑障碍；同伦等价行为依赖于环境Hilbert空间维数：非极小维数下退化为幺正等价，极小维数下涌现依赖于相对相位的相对同伦不变量；最终构建了低维能隙量子系统的统一几何分类框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受拓扑绝缘体启发，旨在建立低维（d ≤ 2）具有Altland-Zirnbauer-Cartan（AZC）对称性（时间反演、粒子-空穴、手征）的投影值映射的系统性分类理论，并厘清不同数学等价关系（Murray-von Neumann、幺正、同伦）所对应的物理可测拓扑不变量。

Method: 基于伪周期性约束构造投影像空间的对称基，分析其绕环面非平凡回路产生的相位响应；利用K-理论与拓扑向量丛方法，结合对称性表示论，区分不同AZC类下的基存在性与相位障碍；通过比较不同等价关系在固定对称性与环境Hilbert空间维数下的实现条件，导出绝对与相对拓扑不变量。

Result: d=1时所有AZC类均允许完全周期对称基，故三类等价一致；d=2时Murray-von Neumann与幺正等价由Chern数（ℤ）或Fu-Kane-Mele不变量（ℤ₂）完全分类；同伦等价在非极小环境空间中等价于幺正等价，而在极小环境空间中需引入相对同伦群π₁(Grₖ(ℂⁿ), O)或π₂，其不变量为相对相位差，仅在幺正共轭下保持良定义。

Conclusion: Murray-von Neumann与幺正等价生成绝对拓扑不变量（ℤ或ℤ₂），而同伦等价在极小维度下仅给出相对拓扑相位——这统一解释了能隙系统中‘整体’与‘相对’拓扑序的数学起源，并确立了对称性、空间维数与环境空间维数三者对拓扑分类的协同决定作用。

Abstract: [An extended abstract is provided in-text]
  Motivated by topological insulators, this paper analyzes classification schemes for projection-valued maps on $d$-dimensional tori, $d \le 2$, subject to Altland-Zirnbauer-Cartan (AZC) symmetries (time-reversal, particle-hole, and chiral). The main objective is to compare three notions of equivalence for these maps: Murray-von Neumann, unitary, and homotopy equivalence. The analysis relies on constructing symmetric bases for the projection ranges under a pseudo-periodicity constraint, where select basis vectors pick up a phase when looped around the torus. If $d=1$, fully periodic symmetric bases exist across all AZC classes. If $d=2$, topological obstructions appear as non-trivial phases characterized by $\mathbb{Z}$ (Chern number) or $\mathbb{Z}_2$ (Fu-Kane-Mele) invariants. These serve as absolute invariants that classify both Murray-von Neumann and unitary equivalences. For homotopy equivalence, the behavior depends on the ambient Hilbert space: if this has non-minimal dimension, homotopy equivalence reduces directly to unitary equivalence, while in minimal ambient spaces additional relative homotopy invariants emerge. Their precise values change under unitary conjugation, leaving only relative topological phases between maps well-defined. Ultimately, this provides a unified geometric framework for gapped quantum systems in low dimensions, demonstrating how distinct equivalence relations generate specific absolute and relative topological invariants.

</details>


### [135] [Poisson eigenvalue statistics and dynamical delocalization for fractional and long-range Anderson models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33084)
*Peter D. Hislop,Rodrigo Matos,Constanza Rojas-Molina*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了长程安德森模型（包括分数阶安德森模型）在强无序 regime 下的局域本征值统计与动力学（去）局域化性质；证明了当 β > d/2 时，本征值统计服从强度为态密度 n(E) 的泊松点过程，且存在动力学去局域化（即不存在动力学局域化），这与最近邻安德森模型形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解长程跃迁对无序系统谱统计和动力学行为的影响，特别是检验 Disertori 等人关于长程模型缺乏动力学局域化的猜想，并厘清分数阶拉普拉斯算子随机扰动下的普适性行为。

Method: 运用分数矩方法（fractional moment method）建立格林函数的显式、尖锐下界，结合泊松极限定理与动力学可及性判据（如位置算符高阶矩发散），分析强无序下谱统计与时间演化性质。

Result: 1) 当 β > d/2 时，局域本征值统计为强度 n(E) 的泊松点过程；2) 强无序下不存在动力学局域化（即动力学去局域化），表现为 ⟨‖X(t)‖^p⟩ → ∞ 对任意 p > 0；3) 特别地，在 d = 1 时，分数阶安德森模型（对应 (−Δ)^α, α ∈ (1/2,1)）亦满足上述结论。

Conclusion: 长程跳跃（衰减指数 β > d/2）足以破坏安德森局域化机制，导致泊松本征值统计与动力学去局域化共存，表明该类模型属于非遍历但非局域化的新型无序量子相。

Abstract: We study local eigenvalue statistics and dynamical (de)-localization properties for long-range Anderson models, including the fractional Anderson model. These systems are random Anderson-type perturbations of operators with long-range (non random) hopping terms of the form $|T(n,m)| \sim \|n-m\|^{-(d+2β)}$ for $β>0$, on the $d$-dimensional lattice. In the presence of a strong enough random potential, these models exhibit dense pure point spectrum with polynomially decaying eigenvectors, almost surely. We show that in this strong disorder regime, and for $β>d/2$, the local eigenvalue statistics centered at any $E$ in the deterministic spectrum is a Poisson point process with intensity given by the density of states function $n(E)$. Moreover, we prove that, at strong disorder, there is no dynamical localization for these models, verifying a conjecture of Disertori et al. We achieve this by establishing explicit, sharp lower bounds on the Green's functions fractional moments which imply that large moments of the position operator are infinite. Hence, these models exhibit Poisson eigenvalue statistics and dynamical delocalization, that is, the absence of dynamical localization. In particular, this shows that in dimension $d=1$, the fractional Anderson model, a random perturbation of the fractional Laplacian $(-Δ)^α$ with $α\in (0,1)$, exhibits Poisson eigenvalue statistics and dynamical delocalization if the disorder is strong and the exponent $\frac{1}{2}<α<1$. This is in stark contrast with what is known for the usual Anderson model that has only nearest-neighbor hopping.

</details>


### [136] [Minimal output entropy for the channels that add or remove a box of a Young diagram, and a Pauli principle for every permutation symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33498)
*Robin Reuvers*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了U(d)群不可约表示间由单个方块差异引起的四种协变量子信道，证明了相干态在这些信道下输出态的主序性（majorization），从而最小化输出熵，并显式计算了最优输出（用hook长度表示）；进一步确定了两类保留方块信道的所有熵最小化输入态（未必是相干态）；最后将结果应用于具有给定置换对称性的N粒子系统，给出了单粒子约化密度矩阵谱的精确界及可达谱集（含Pauli不相容原理与Coleman定理的推广）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解协变量子信道下的熵极值问题，特别是与群表示论和量子关联结构相关的主序性与熵最小化问题；为多体量子系统的单粒子约化谱提供严格刻画。

Method: 运用U(d)群表示论、Young图组合学、majorization理论、协变信道分析及凸分析方法；关键工具包括hook长度公式、Schur-Weyl对偶性、以及对称张量空间的分解。

Result: 1) 四类协变信道中，相干态输出主序于所有其他输入态输出；2) 输出熵在相干态处取最小值，且最优输出本征值由hook长度显式给出；3) 对两类‘保留盒子’信道，完全刻画了所有熵最小化输入态（含非相干纯态）；4) 对N粒子置换对称系统，给出单粒子约化谱的sharp边界及混合态可达谱集（fermionic情形即Pauli原理与Coleman定理）。

Conclusion: 相干态在特定协变信道下实现熵极小化具有普适性，其主序性源于U(d)表示的组合结构；该框架统一并推广了多体量子约束（如Pauli原理），揭示了对称性、熵与谱几何之间的深层联系。

Abstract: Two irreducible representations of $U(d)$ whose Young diagrams differ by a single box are connected by four covariant quantum channels: the box can be removed or added, and one keeps either the new diagram or the box ($\mathbb{C}^d$ or its dual). We prove that for each of these channels the output of a coherent state majorizes every other output, so that coherent states minimize the output entropy, and we compute the optimal output explicitly in terms of hook lengths. For the two channels that keep the box we also determine all minimizers; these need not be coherent, even when they are pure. As an application we consider $N$ particles with a given permutation symmetry and find sharp bounds on the spectrum of the reduced density matrix of a single particle, as well as the exact set of spectra that mixed states can reach (for fermions these are the Pauli principle and Coleman's theorem).

</details>


### [137] [Effective atom-dimer bands of three-body lattice systems at strong coupling: exact critical mass ratios and a statistics-quasimomentum duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33576)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破以往仅限于零总准动量和三聚体态的研究局限，系统刻画全布里渊区上三粒子体系的原子-二聚体连续谱阈值结构，揭示强关联系统中统计、动量与质量比之间的深层耦合关系。

Method: 采用Feshbach-Schur约化技术，将希尔伯特空间投影至两体接触算符本征值1的无穷简并子空间，导出带有一阶误差控制的有效哈密顿量；阈值分析转化为由正方晶格两点电阻构成的4×4厄米矩阵判据，并进一步约化为四次多项式求根问题；结合格点电导理论、符号计算、精确对角化与无穷晶格求解器进行交叉验证。

Conclusion: 三粒子晶格接触模型在全布里渊区展现出丰富的普适结构，其阈值几何完全由晶格电阻决定，且具有严格的解析可解性与多重对偶性；该体系为理解强关联多体阈值物理、离散对称性破缺及格点RG流提供了基准范例。

Abstract: We study three particles on the square lattice with contact interactions of infinite strength, for all total quasimomenta, for three statistics: three identical bosons, two identical fermions plus a third particle, and two identical bosons plus a third particle. Previous work treated only the trimer and zero quasimomentum. Here the atom-dimer sector is analyzed on the whole Brillouin zone. A Feshbach-Schur reduction onto the infinitely degenerate eigenvalue 1 of the pair-contact operator gives explicit effective Hamiltonians with proved first-order error. The threshold problem reduces to a 4x4 Hermitian matrix criterion built from the two-point resistances of the square lattice, and hence to a quartic polynomial. This yields the complete atom-dimer zone and the critical mass ratio on the Brillouin zone, with bound between 1 and pi/(pi-2), and closed forms at all symmetry points. We prove: the zones in closed form; the critical mass ratio with exact value pi/(pi-2) at zero quasimomentum, reproducing a numerical value; at most two atom-dimer states for every quasimomentum and mass ratio; the onset laws, an essential singularity on the zone boundary and a linear-over-logarithm onset in the pure p-wave; two exact dualities showing that a change of statistics is equivalent to a shift of quasimomentum by pi; and the global range of the second fermionic threshold. The onset laws admit a renormalization-group reading: the inverse lattice Green function is a marginal s-wave coupling, binding energies arise by dimensional transmutation with lattice scale 16, and the zone boundary is of marginal (BCS/Kondo) type rather than of Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless type. All constants are verified independently by lattice quadrature, computer algebra, exact diagonalisation, and infinite-lattice solvers.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于有界算子的预解式，通过逆Stieltjes变换构造广义分布——p-进谱分布；由此定义玻色型与费米型p-进zeta函数；运用p-进分析、分布理论与谱理论推导其解析延拓、特殊值及渐近展开；验证离散谱情形为其特例。

Result: 确立了连续谱p-进谱分布的存在性与唯一性；定义并解析延拓了玻色/费米p-进zeta函数；获得其在s=0等关键点的特殊值公式及p-进Stirling型渐近展开；证明该框架自然包含先前的离散谱理论；发现玻色与费米zeta函数在p-进设置下呈现精确的函数方程对称性。

Conclusion: p-进谱zeta函数理论可系统地推广至连续谱，为p-进量子力学与非阿基米德场论中的算子正则化提供了新工具；玻色/费米对称性暗示了p-进世界中统计性质的深层几何或代数起源。

Abstract: Spectral zeta functions provide a standard tool for regularizing determinants of differential operators in quantum physics. In a previous paper (J. Math. Phys. 66: 083505, 2025), we introduced a $p$-adic spectral zeta function for discrete spectra via a locally analytic interpolation function. In this paper we extend the framework to continuous spectra using the inverse Stieltjes transform. Starting from the resolvent of a bounded operator, we construct a generalized distribution, called the $p$-adic spectral distribution, and define bosonic and fermionic zeta functions and functional determinants. We establish their analytic properties, special value formulas, and Stirling expansions, and show that our previous framework is embedded into the present one in the discrete case. In this approach, the bosonic and fermionic cases exhibit an interesting symmetry. As an application, we consider the position operator in $p$-adic quantum mechanics on an arbitrary compact subset of $\mathbb{C}_{p}$.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用图上的均匀化理论，结合傅里叶插值将度量图上薛定谔型算子（ODE）映射至欧氏空间中有效PDE算子；通过同步嵌入并耦合两个周期图构造具有内部自由度的有效介质；利用谱分解与正交杂化态空间分析，结合高对比度极限下的算子收敛性（特别是约化预解式的O(ε)算子范数估计）刻画BIC的生成与稳定性。

Conclusion: 高对比度耦合图结构可作为实现BIC的普适几何-分析框架；其物理本质源于微观共振在均匀化极限下的非平凡保留，以及由此诱导的杂化谱结构干涉；该机制不依赖于对称性保护，属衍射型BIC的严格数学实现。

Abstract: By homogenizing high-contrast periodic metric graphs, we create continuous media that admit bound states produced by local defects at energies embedded in the continuous spectrum. This is achieved by fitting two copies of a lattice graph simultaneously into space and coupling them by connecting edges. The homogenization of an ODE on a graph to a PDE in space is achieved by representing the associated operators in a common functional space through interpolation by Fourier analysis. The two coupled graphs result in an effective medium with internal degrees of freedom that allow a decomposition into two orthogonal spaces of hybrid states. Critical high contrast retains microscopic resonances in the effective medium and allows a spectral band of one hybrid state space to overlap a spectral gap of the other. This serves as the mechanism for creating bound states at frequencies embedded in the continuous spectrum of the effective medium. These bound states in the continuum persist for $\varepsilon$-scale metric graphs due to $O(\varepsilon)$ operator-norm estimates for reduced resolvents on the defect region.

</details>

*Hal Tasaki*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了Ogata算子代数指标与Tasaki扭指数在整数自旋链、具有U(1)⋊ℤ₂对称性及对称局域唯一能隙基态条件下等价，并给出显式误差界与可计算公式；作为应用，确定了反铁磁海森堡链的Ogata指标为(-1)^S。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一两类SPT相拓扑指标：Ogata指标适用于广义对称类但缺乏物理直观，Tasaki扭指数依赖U(1)连续对称性但具清晰物理解释；需在共同适用情形下建立严格等价性。

Method: 通过电荷涨落估计构造半链旋转算符的强极限并固定其相位；利用精确群交换子恒等式将Ogata指标表为局域扭期望值极限；结合谱隙下界与基态期望值导出显式误差界。

Result: 在整数自旋链、U(1)⋊ℤ₂对称性、对称局域唯一能隙基态（无需平移不变性或MPS表示）下，Ogata指标等于Tasaki扭指数极限；获得带显式误差界的可计算公式；推得反铁磁海森堡链Ogata指标为(-1)^S。

Conclusion: Ogata算子代数指标与Tasaki扭指数在物理相关设定下严格等价，前者获得明确物理解释与数值可行性；该等价性拓展了SPT相指标的适用范围并强化了其鲁棒性。

Abstract: Symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phases in quantum spin chains are distinguished by topological indices. Ogata's operator-algebraic indices apply to a broad range of symmetry classes without requiring continuous symmetry, whereas the elementary twist index of Tasaki requires $\mathrm{U}(1)$ symmetry. We prove their equivalence in the common setting of integer-spin chains with on-site $\mathrm{U}(1)\rtimes\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry and a symmetric locally-unique gapped ground state, without assuming translation invariance or a matrix product representation. A charge-fluctuation estimate yields a strong-limit construction of half-chain rotations with their phases fixed. An exact group commutator identity then identifies the Ogata index with the limit of local twist expectations and gives an explicit error bound. This provides a computable formula when a lower bound on the gap and the relevant ground-state expectations are available. As an application, a previous twist-index calculation determines the Ogata index to be $(-1)^S$ for the antiferromagnetic Heisenberg chain under explicit uniqueness and boundary-field gap assumptions.
  The author formulated the problem and proposed the basic strategy, while ChatGPT made substantial contributions to the development of the proof.

</details>


### [141] [Discontinuity of Continuum Fermion Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34402)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 运用C*-代数动力学框架，结合自动连续性定理（automatic continuity theorem）与CAR代数的规范不变子代数结构分析，构造反例并证明范数不连续性。

Result: 证明了在非恒定对势下，海森堡动力学在规范不变CAR代数上不满足逐点范数连续性；进而推得CAR代数及其规范不变子代数均非动力学不变，且非不变性发生在Lebesgue几乎处处时刻。

Conclusion: 标准C*-代数动力学框架中隐含的范数连续性假设在物理相关的连续费米系统中可能失效，提示需发展更广义的动力学描述（如W*-代数框架或非强连续群表示）。

Abstract: We study the Heisenberg dynamics of continuum fermions in $\IR^d$ interacting through a pair potential. For a large class of nonconstant pair potentials and under suitable assumptions on the dispersion relation and external potential, we prove that the dynamics fails to be pointwise-norm continuous even on the gauge-invariant CAR algebra. An automatic-continuity argument then shows that neither the CAR algebra nor its gauge-invariant subalgebra is invariant under the dynamics, with non-invariance occurring for almost all times.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为构建量子场论中协变、几何化的有效作用量理论，需发展一种在纤维化或降维背景下保持BV代数结构的推前操作。经典BV pushforward旨在系统化处理约束、对称性约化及背景场依赖下的有效动力学。

Method: 基于Batalin–Vilkovisky (BV) 代数框架，利用微分几何中的纤维积分与同伦代数技术，严格构造经典BV pushforward算子；通过分析梯度向量场、Hessian非退化性及BV拉普拉斯算子的协变性，验证其对CME解和BV规范等价类的保持性。

Result: 确立了经典BV pushforward作为BV复形间的链映射，证明其在CME解空间上诱导良定义的映射，并在孤立临界点假设下保持BV规范等价类；该构造兼容经典极限下的量子pushforward预期行为。

Conclusion: 经典BV pushforward为经典场论的对称性约化与有效描述提供了严格数学工具，是通向量子BV pushforward及背景无关量子引力形式化的重要中间步骤。

Abstract: We define the classical BV pushforward and establish its two main properties. First, we prove that it maps solutions of the classical master equation (CME) on the total space to solutions of the CME on the smaller space. Second, we prove that, for isolated critical points, it maps BV canonically equivalent solutions to BV canonically equivalent solutions. We illustrate the construction with examples and discuss its applications.

</details>


### [143] [Scattering Measures and Information Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34721)
*Tatsuro Oda*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将散射实验建模为参数化的有限测度，并基于Fisher度量构建其信息几何；通过分解总质量与归一化形状，分离出散射强度变化与结果分布重排各自对Fisher信息的贡献；测量过程被表示为作用于散射测度的概率或次概率核，这些核具有信息收缩性；该框架统一了实验操作的信息论描述，并将散射实验与最优实验设计理论联系起来；同时指出该有限测度结构自然出现在探测不完全的量子测量中，从而厘清了从量子测量到经典统计结果的层次关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一个统一、几何化且信息论自洽的散射实验数学框架，以桥接高能物理实验建模、统计推断与量子测量理论。

Method: 采用参数化有限测度（而非传统概率测度）刻画散射结果分布；引入Fisher信息度量定义其信息几何；对测度进行Radon–Nikodym分解（总质量 × 归一化密度）；将测量过程建模为Markov核（sub-probability kernel）作用；利用信息收缩定理（information contraction inequality）分析核对Fisher信息的影响。

Result: 证明Fisher信息可正交分解为强度项（mass variation）与形状项（shape variation）；任意测量核导致Fisher信息非增（contraction）；散射测度的信息几何与最优实验设计中的D-最优性等准则直接对应；探测效率η < 1 的POVM诱导的古典统计恰对应一类带衰减因子的子概率核作用。

Conclusion: 散射实验的本质结构是参数化有限测度，其信息几何由Fisher度量完全刻画；该结构既是经典散射统计的自然基础，也是不完备量子测量通向经典结果的严格中间层；由此可在统一框架下比较不同实验配置的信息效率，并为高能物理中的探测器优化与参数灵敏度分析提供微分几何工具。

Abstract: We formulate scattering experiments in terms of parameterized finite measures on outcome spaces and develop the associated information geometry defined by the Fisher metric. The decomposition of a scattering measure into total mass and normalized shape separates Fisher information into contributions from changes in overall scattering strength and redistribution among outcomes. Measurement processes are represented by probability or sub-probability kernels acting on the scattering measure. Such kernels contract Fisher information, placing a broad class of experimental operations within a common information-theoretic framework. This formulation connects scattering experiments to the theory of optimal experimental design through the Fisher geometry of scattering measures. The same finite-measure structure arises naturally in quantum measurements with incomplete detection, clarifying its place in the hierarchy from quantum measurement to classical outcome statistics.

</details>


### [144] [Notes on Magnetostatic Interactions in $\mathbb R^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34766)
*Vladimir Gol'dshtein,Wolfgang H. Müller,Reuven Segev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不依赖本构关系（包括以太关系）的静磁学紧凑表述，通过引入磁偶极子分布的电流密度势场来定义势能，并基于虚功原理导出力学力泛函，其中包含洛伦兹力、开尔文力、非对称主动应力张量（其反对称部分对应力学力偶密度）及压力项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立不依赖传统本构关系（尤其是有争议的以太关系）的静磁学基本表述，统一处理磁偶极子体系的势能与力学响应。

Method: 基于磁偶极子分布由电流密度势场刻画的假设，构造区域势能泛函；应用虚功原理（虚功等于势能时间导数的负值），变分推导力学力泛函；对力密度进行张量分解，分离出对称与反对称部分。

Result: 导出含洛伦兹力密度、开尔文力密度、非对称主动应力张量（含力学力偶密度作为其反对称部）及压力项的广义力泛函；表明静磁相互作用中存在固有耦合的力与力偶响应。

Conclusion: 静磁学可被重构为一个仅依赖矢量势与电流密度势场的几何/变分理论，无需预设磁化强度与磁场间的本构关系；所得力泛函揭示了磁介质中应力-偶极矩耦合的内在结构。

Abstract: A compact formulation of magnetostatics is presented. Without using constitutive relations, including the aether relations, an expression is proposed for the potential energy of a region carrying a distribution of magnetic dipoles under a vector potential field, where the dipole distribution is specified by a current density potential field. Assuming that during a virtual motion of the dipole distribution the virtual work expended by the field is equal to minus the time derivative of the potential energy, an expression for the mechanical force functional is derived. In addition to the Lorentz and Kelvin force densities, the force functional contains an asymmetric active stress field, the skew-symmetric part of which is the mechanical couple density, and a pressure term.

</details>


### [145] [A renormalized Lee Model: resolvent and spectral analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34980)
*Claudio Cacciapuoti,Federica Muscolino,Diego Noja,Andrea Posilicano*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建并分析了一个三维有质量Lee模型的重正化版本，通过抽象构造明确定义了其重正化哈密顿量的算子定义域、作用方式及Krein型预解式；利用总激发数守恒将全哈密顿量谱分析约化为各激发扇区上的算子 pencil 谱剖面研究；证明了扇区化HVZ型本质谱公式（在前两个扇区严格成立，其余扇区在附加条件下成立）；在自由/单激发阈值以下，高扇区点谱由一个非负自伴Birman–Schwinger算子刻画；并在多激发扇区中给出了亚阈值谱空缺与束缚态存在的精确条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立严格数学框架下三维有质量Lee模型的重正化哈密顿量，克服紫外发散问题，并系统研究其谱结构（特别是本质谱与束缚态），以深化对非相对论量子场论中强耦合、低维重整化模型的理解。

Method: 采用抽象算子理论方法：通过形式二次型的闭包与Friedrichs扩张定义重正化哈密顿量；利用总激发数守恒实现扇区分解；引入算子值Weyl函数（即激发扇区间耦合的解析延拓工具）；结合Krein型预解式、HVZ定理推广、Birman–Schwinger原理及变分估计进行谱分析。

Result: 1) 构造出具有良好定义域与Krein型预解式的重正化哈密顿量；2) 证明扇区化HVZ型本质谱公式（前两扇区普适，高扇区需附加条件）；3) 在亚阈值区域，各高扇区点谱由有界非负自伴Birman–Schwinger算子完全刻画；4) 对≥2激发扇区，给出亚阈值谱空缺的充分条件（含强/弱耦合两类情形）；5) 在弱耦合且重正化能隙小于玻色子质量时，导出两激发扇区存在简单束缚态的充分判据。

Conclusion: 该工作为三维有质量Lee模型提供了首个严格重正化构造与完整谱分析框架，揭示了激发扇区间耦合对本质谱与束缚态形成的精细调控机制，为非微扰QFT中重正化哈密顿量的谱理论研究树立了新范式。

Abstract: We construct and analyze a renormalized three-dimensional massive Lee model; its renormalized Hamiltonian is defined intrinsically through an abstract construction which explicitly provides the operator domain, action and a Kre\uın-like resolvent formula. Conservation of the total excitation number reduces the spectral analysis of the full Hamiltonian to a sector-wise study of the spectral profile of an explicit operator pencil, playing the role of an operator-valued Weyl function. We construct essential spectrum components in a sector generated by lower excitation sectors and obtain the inclusion in the expected sector-wise HVZ type formula; the full formula is proved in the first two sectors and, under explicit conditions, in every sector. Below the free or one-excitation threshold, the point spectrum in every higher sector is described by a bounded self-adjoint nonnegative Birman--Schwinger operator. In every sector with at least two excitations we prove absence of sub-threshold spectrum for arbitrary coupling when the renormalized gap is not smaller than the boson mass, and under an explicit weak-coupling condition when it is smaller. In the latter case we also derive a sufficient condition for the existence of a simple bound state in the two-excitation sector.

</details>


### [146] [GR-FM: Geometrically Regularized Flow Matching for SDF-Based Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35006)
*Yuxin Ai,Zhichang Guo,Fanghui Song,Dazhi Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何正则化流匹配（GR-FM）的医学图像分割框架，利用符号距离场（SDF）建模目标结构，结合双调和正则项与Eikonal约束提升空间平滑性、结构一致性和距离场特性，并通过连续概率输运实现高精度边界定位与复杂结构保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Flow Matching的生成式分割方法缺乏对重建表示的空间正则性与距离场特性的显式约束，易导致空间振荡、边界偏移及细结构不连续；同时医学图像常存在弱边界、不规则形状与标注稀缺等问题。

Method: 提出图像条件下的几何正则化流匹配（GR-FM）框架：以SDF替代二值掩膜作为隐式表征，将分割建模为从初始分布到SDF目标分布的连续概率输运过程；采用Flow Matching与ODE实现确定性高效输运；引入biharmonic正则项与Eikonal约束联合优化；理论分析零水平集演化与几何约束下的输运动力学。

Result: 在MoNuSeg、GlaS和DRIVE数据集上，GR-FM在区域重叠（如Dice）与边界精度（如HD95）指标上达到SOTA或竞争性性能，显著降低结果方差，并在极少积分步数下保持稳定分割。

Conclusion: SDF表征与几何感知的流匹配建模可有效缓解医学图像分割中边界模糊与结构失真问题；显式几何约束是提升隐式表征保真度与泛化鲁棒性的关键设计。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation remains challenging in terms of accurate boundary localization and complex-structure preservation, as target regions often exhibit weak boundaries, fine-grained structures, and irregular shapes, while high-quality images and precise annotations are usually limited. Existing generative segmentation approaches based on Flow Matching mainly learn velocity fields in the state space but lack explicit constraints on the spatial regularity and distance-field properties of the recovered representation. This limitation may lead to spatial oscillations, boundary displacement, and discontinuities in fine structures. To address these issues, we propose an image-conditioned Geometrically Regularized Flow Matching framework, termed GR-FM, which formulates segmentation as a continuous probability transport process from an initial distribution to a target implicit representation distribution. Instead of directly modeling binary masks, GR-FM adopts the SDF to describe the target structure, where each pixel is represented by its signed distance to the object boundary, resulting in a continuous geometric field. Flow Matching and ordinary differential equations are then employed to achieve deterministic and efficient distribution transport. The training objective further incorporates a biharmonic regularization term and an Eikonal constraint to enhance the spatial smoothness, structural consistency, and distance-field characteristics of the recovered representation. Moreover, we analyze the continuous transport process under geometric constraints and investigate the evolution of the zero level set. Experiments conducted on the MoNuSeg, GlaS, and DRIVE datasets demonstrate that GR-FM achieves competitive performance in both region overlap and boundary accuracy, reduces performance variation, and maintains stable segmentation results with only a small number of integration steps.

</details>
